# A/R and A/P Report Sorting: Preconditions rewritten as a scenario (26 Oct 2026)

QA lead, 26 Oct 2026: *"Preconditions should be like a scenario, easier for a manual QA tester to understand  to create in the account."* (Rule 117 thirteenth amendment; lesson L-10.) Draft only: nothing written to TestRail, Jira, Slack or Google; nothing committed.

## Result
- Cases: 357. Every case now opens its Preconditions with one hand-written "Scenario:" line (357 of 357).
- Other wording changes: the standard Admin line now reads "You are an Admin user (the "Admin" role) working at {Location-A}, your current location." (was "You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, with {Location-A} as your current location."), in every case that uses it; the Location-A example line is unchanged. Cases whose remaining preconditions are otherwise untouched: 356 of 357.
- Cases with any other precondition change: 1 (S5-39). S5-39: the old first line packed the organization into the role sentence ("in a test organization that has 8 vendors or fewer"); it is now the standard role line plus its own bullet, "Your organization has 8 vendors or fewer in total.", with the same example values split across the two "For example:" lines.
- Nothing found to remove: no precondition said who to ask, named the QA lead, or used a generic line such as a test organization set aside for a suite (script scan of all 357 cases before the pass).
- The Blocked line stays last in every case; the setup-file link above the Preconditions is unchanged.

## Byte-identity check (every field other than custom_preconds)
- Compared: title, refs, sections, reports, requirement codes and every TestRail field (template, type, priority, automation fields, custom_steps, custom_expected) for all 357 cases, before vs after.
- Cases where anything other than custom_preconds changed: 0 (none).
- Structural problems: 0 (none).
- Before file: `proposals-before-committed.json` (the committed `authoring/proposals.json`; re-running build.py before the pass reproduced it byte for byte).

## Gates
- `authoring/build.py` gains the scenario gate: exactly one "Scenario:" line, first in the Preconditions, at most 40 words, no requirement code, placeholder, "QA lead" or "ask your". The scenario text is also checked by the existing tester-text checks (no requirement code, no technical term, no hard-coded test record). Negative test run: six planted faults all reported, then restored.
- Setup file `AR-AP-SETUP-FILE.md` regenerated: each recipe's "Preconditions, exactly as the case states them" list now starts with the Scenario line; the only other changed lines are the Admin wording (341) and S5-39's new bullet. `render_outputs.py` re-run: REPORT.md, the ledger, sweep, PO draft, checklist and divergence register are unchanged.
